  a.prototype.check = function (b) {
    if (
      Number(game.player.getBase().energy) >= 30 ||
      (game.player.isTest == true && Number(game.player.getBase().energy) >= 1)
    )
      return 0;

    else {
      var c = game.player.getBase().arenaData.split(",");

      if (b == 1) {
        b = game.player.getPropById(36);

        if (b == void 0) return -1;

        c = parseInt(c[0]) + 1;

        if (c >= 31) return -2;

        c = this.accountNum(Math.ceil(c / 3));

        return b.num >= c ? c : -1;

      } else if (b == 4) {
        b = game.player.getPropById(39);

        if (b == void 0) return -1;

        c = parseInt(c[1]) + 1;

        if (c >= 31) return -2;

        c = this.accountNum(Math.ceil(c / 3));

        return b.num >= c ? c : -1;

      }
    }
  };

  a.prototype.accountNum = function (b) {
    return b == 1
      ? 1
      : b == 2
        ? 2
        : b > 2
          ? this.accountNum(b - 1) + this.accountNum(b - 2)
          : 0;

  };

            : game.alert(b.msg);

        });

    });

  };

    b == "arena"

    d = null;

  a.prototype.update = function () {
    if (game.arena.getPlayers().length != this.playerLength)
      ((this.playerLength = game.arena.getPlayers().length),
        this.cleanRubbish(),
        this.drawPlayer());

    if (this.isCheck)
      if (game.arena.getStatus() == 1)
        ((this.isReady = 1), (this.isCheck = false));

      else if (game.arena.getStatus() == 2)
        ((this.isReady = 0),
          (this.isCheck = false),
          game.alert(
            "\u5339\u914d\u5931\u8d25\uff0c\u8bf7\u7a0d\u540e\u91cd\u8bd5\u3002",
            function () {

              game.joinArena = null;

              var b = new Arena({
                id: "arena",
                x: 0,
                y: 0,
                width: stage.width,
                height: stage.height,
              });

              stage.addChild(b);

            },
          ));

    if (this.isReady == 1)
      (game.showWaiting(true), (this.counter = true), (this.isReady = 2));

    else if (
      this.isReady == 2 &&
      (this.loadingTime++, this.loadingTime == 10)
    ) {
      game.fightInfo = new FightInfo(3);

      game.fightInfo.setFightingReward(game.arena.getFirstfightingProp());

      game.fightInfo.setFightData(game.arena.getFirstCombat());

      var b = game.player.getBase().arenaData.split(",");

      if (game.player.arenaUseProp == 1 && this.counter == true)
        ((this.counter = false),
          game.player.isTest == true
            ? (game.player.getBase().energy = Math.max(
                0,
                game.player.getBase().energy - 1,
              ))
            : (game.player.getBase().energy = Math.max(
                0,
                game.player.getBase().energy - 30,
              )));

      else if (game.player.arenaUseProp == 2 && this.counter == true) {
        this.counter = false;

        var c = this.accountNum(Math.ceil((parseInt(b[0]) + 1) / 3));

        game.player.deleteProps({ id: 36, count: c });

        b[0] = parseInt(b[0]) + 1;

        game.player.getBase().arenaData = "" + b[0] + "," + b[1];

      } else if (game.player.arenaUseProp == 3 && this.counter == true)
        ((this.counter = false),
          (c = this.accountNum(Math.ceil((parseInt(b[1]) + 1) / 3))),
          game.player.deleteProps({ id: 39, count: c }),
          (b[1] = parseInt(b[1]) + 1),
          (game.player.getBase().arenaData = "" + b[0] + "," + b[1]));

      game.chooseFightBgSet();

      game.loadRes(game.loadArr, function () {
        game.loadArr = [];

        game.showWaiting(false);

        game.player.arenaUseProp = 0;

        game.joinArena = null;

        game.state = STATE.FIGHT;

      });

    }
  };

(function () {
  var a = function (b) {
    a.superClass.constructor.call(this, b);

    this.layer = [];

    this.bgWidth = [];

    this.regPointX = [];

    this.effect_layer = [];

    this.effect_cache = [];

    this.current_Frame = [];

    this.point = [];

    for (var c in game.current_FightSet.layer) {
      if (game.current_FightSet.layer[c].image != "")
        ((this.layer[c] = BitmapFactory.createBitmap({
          id: "backB",
          src: game.current_FightSet.layer[c].image,
          label: "",
          x: game.current_FightSet.layer[c].x,
          y: game.current_FightSet.layer[c].y,
        })),
          (this.bgWidth[c] = Math.round(this.layer[c].rectWidth)),
          (this.layer[c].rectX = Math.round(
            (this.bgWidth[c] -
              this.bgWidth[c] / game.current_FightSet.layer[c].scaleX) /
              2,
          )),
          (this.layer[c].rectY = 0),
          (this.layer[c].rectWidth = Math.round(
            game.current_FightSet.layer[c].width /
              game.current_FightSet.layer[c].scaleX,
          )),
          (this.layer[c].width = Math.round(
            game.current_FightSet.layer[c].width /
              game.current_FightSet.layer[c].scaleX,
          )),
          (this.layer[c].scaleX = game.current_FightSet.layer[c].scaleX),
          (this.layer[c].scaleY = game.current_FightSet.layer[c].scaleY),
          (this.regPointX[c] = Math.round(
            (this.bgWidth[c] -
              game.current_FightSet.layer[c].width /
                game.current_FightSet.layer[c].scaleX) /
              2,
          )),
          (this.point[c] = Math.round(game.current_FightSet.layer[c].y)));

      game.current_FightSet.layer[c].effectData != "" &&
        ((this.effect_layer[c] = AnimationData.parseData(
          game.current_FightSet.layer[c].effectData,
        )),
        (this.effect_cache[c] = new BitmapCache(
          [game.current_FightSet.layer[c].src],
          "all",
        )),
        (this.current_Frame[c] = 0));

    }
    game.isMoveBg = false;

    game.moveSet = game.current_FightSet.MapData;

    this.vector = { x: [], y: [] };

    for (var d in game.current_FightSet.MapData_ini.x)
      ((this.vector.x[d] = game.current_FightSet.MapData_ini.x[d]),
        (this.vector.y[d] = game.current_FightSet.MapData_ini.y[d]));

    this.shakeCounter = 0;

    this.startShake = false;

    this.bgRubbish = [];

    this.counter = 0;

    this.resArr = [];

    game.animationArray = [];

    this.rubbish = [];

    game.fightController = new FightController();

    game.fightRound = new RoundAnimation();

    this.bloodStyle = this.getStyle();

    this.fightingTimes = 0;

    this.fightingPropList = [];

    this.isAlertEndWindow = true;

    this.index = -1;

    this.isStart = this.isSkip = false;

    this.startAttHp = game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp;

    this.startDefHp = game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp;

    this.noFlashProp = false;

    if (game.fightInfo.getFightingReward() == void 0) this.noFlashProp = true;

    game.npcFight != void 0
      ? game.npcFight.startIndex == 1 &&
        game.fightInfo.getAttacker().playerId != game.player.getBase().playerId
        ? ((this.talk = 1), this.take(this.init()))
        : this.init()
      : game.fightInfo.getType() == 7 &&
          game.fightInfo.getAttacker().playerId !=
            game.player.getBase().playerId
        ? ((this.talk = 2), this.take2(this.init()))
        : ((this.talk = 0), this.init());

  };

  Q.inherit(a, Q.DisplayObjectContainer);

  var d = [];

  a.prototype.shakeBg = function (b) {
    var c = b % 2 == 0 ? 10 : -10;

    if (b == 4) {
      for (b in game.current_FightSet.layer)
        if (this.layer[b] != void 0) this.layer[b].y = this.point[b];

      this.fightEffect();

    } else {
      for (b in game.current_FightSet.layer)
        if (this.layer[b] != void 0) this.layer[b].y = this.point[b] + c;

      this.fightEffect(c);

    }
  };

  a.prototype.updateVector = function () {
    for (var b in game.current_FightSet.layer)
      ((this.vector.x[b] += game.moveSet.x[b]),
        (this.vector.y[b] += game.moveSet.y[b]));

  };

  a.prototype.moveBg = function () {};

  a.prototype.init = function () {
    this.fightEffect(0);

    this.go();

    this.drawFrame();

    this.drawName();

    this.drawRest();

    this.drawBlood();

    this.drawHead();

    this.drawSqu();

    (game.fightInfo.getType() == 0 || game.fightInfo.getType() == 1) &&
      this.drawUseProp();

    if (game.player.newbie == 2) {
      var b = this,
        c = BitmapFactory.createBitmap({
          id: "zimukuang",
          x: stage.width / 2,
          y: stage.height / 2 - 150,
          src: "resource_13",
          label: "0",
          center: true,

      this.addChild(c);

      var a = BitmapFactory.createBitmap({
        id: "zimutext",
        x: stage.width / 2,
        y: stage.height / 2 - 150,
        src: "new",
        label: "" + game.player.newbie,
        center: true,
      });

      this.addChild(a);

      setTimeout(function () {
        b.removeChild(c);

        b.removeChild(a);

      }, 3e3);

      game.player.newbie += 1;

    }
  };

  a.prototype.remove = function (b) {
    for (var c in b) this.contains(b[c]) && this.removeChild(b[c]);

  };

  a.prototype.drawUseProp = function () {
    this.proparr = game.player.getActiveStates();

    for (
      var b = Math.min(3, this.proparr.length), c = [228, 301, 378], a = 0;

      a < b;

      a++
    ) {

        id: "propimg",
        src: "daoju",

        scaleY: 0.35,
      });

      d.x = c[a];

      d.y = 121;

      this.addChild(d);

      this.bgRubbish.push(d);

    }
    game.player.updateState();

  };

  a.prototype.drawUseProp2 = function () {
    for (

      d.y = 121;

      this.bgRubbish.push(d);

    }
  };

  a.prototype.drawRest = function () {
    this.rest = new Rest(true);

    this.rest.currentFrame = 0;

    game.animationArray[0] = this.rest;

    var b = parseInt(game.fightInfo.getDefender().playerId);

    if (b > 1e3 || b < 99 || b == 300)
      ((this.rest1 = new Rest(false)),
        (this.rest1.currentFrame = 0),
        (game.animationArray[1] = this.rest1));

    else if (b > 117 && b < 136)
      ((this.rest1 = new xh_rest()),
        (this.rest1.currentFrame = 0),
        (game.animationArray[1] = this.rest1));

    else if (b > 135 && b < 154)
      ((this.rest1 = new xm_rest()),
        (this.rest1.currentFrame = 0),
        (game.animationArray[1] = this.rest1));

    else if (b > 99 && b < 118)
      ((this.rest1 = new tl_rest()),
        (this.rest1.currentFrame = 0),
        (game.animationArray[1] = this.rest1));

  };

  a.prototype.drawHead = function () {
    this.head_Left = BitmapFactory.createBitmap({
      id: "squirrel1",

      y: 15,

      label: "3",
    });

    this.addChild(this.head_Left);

    this.bgRubbish.push(this.head_Left);

    var b = "3",
      c = -1,
      a = parseInt(game.fightInfo.getDefender().playerId);

    game.fightInfo.getType() == 5 &&
      (a < 118 && a > 99
        ? (b = "4")
        : a > 117 && a < 136
          ? (b = "5")
          : a > 135 && a < 154 && (b = "6"),
      (c *= -1));

    this.head_Right = BitmapFactory.createBitmap({
      id: "squirrel2",
      x: 1001,
      y: 10,
      src: "resource_12",
      label: b,
    });

    this.head_Right.x =
      c == -1 ? this.head_Right.x + this.head_Right.width : this.head_Right.x;

    if (a < 118 && a > 99)
      ((this.head_Right.x = 1011), (this.head_Right.y = 0));

    else if (a > 117 && a < 136)
      ((this.head_Right.x = 990), (this.head_Right.y = 0));

    else if (a > 135 && a < 154)
      ((this.head_Right.x = 1011), (this.head_Right.y = 11));

    this.head_Right.scaleX = c;

    this.addChild(this.head_Right);

    this.bgRubbish.push(this.head_Right);

  };

  a.prototype.update = function () {
    this.clearRubbish();

    if (!game.fightInfo.animationEnd) {
      game.isMoveBg == true && this.updateVector();

      this.fightEffect();

      if (
        this.startShake == true &&
        this.shakeCounter < 5 &&
        (this.shakeBg(this.shakeCounter),
        this.shakeCounter++,
        this.shakeCounter == 5)
      )
        ((this.shakeCounter = 0), (this.startShake = false));

      this.drawFrame();

      this.drawBlood();

      this.drawHead();

      this.drawName();

      (game.fightInfo.getType() == 0 || game.fightInfo.getType() == 1) &&
        this.drawUseProp2();

      this.viewBlood();

      game.fightInfo.animationEnd || this.drawSqu();

      this.isStart ||
        (this.talk == 1 ? this.take() : this.talk == 2 && this.take2(),
        this.bitmap != void 0 &&
          (this.addChild(this.bitmap), this.bgRubbish.push(this.bitmap)));

      game.fightInfo.getFightingReward() != void 0 &&
        game.fightInfo.getFightingReward() != "" &&
        this.fightingProp();

      this.isEnd();

      !this.isSkip && !game.fightInfo.resultView && this.drawSkipButton();

      if (game.fightInfo.last == true) game.fightInfo.last = false;

      game.fightRound.isOver && this.fightNextRound();

    }
  };

  a.prototype.clearRubbish = function () {
    for (var b = 0;
 b < this.rubbish.length;
 b++)
      (this.removeChild(this.rubbish[b]), (this.rubbish[b] = null));

    this.rubbish = null;

    this.rubbish = [];

    for (b = 0;
 b < this.bgRubbish.length;
 b++)
      (this.removeChild(this.bgRubbish[b]), (this.bgRubbish[b] = null));

    this.bgRubbish = null;

    this.bgRubbish = [];

  };

  a.prototype.drawSqu = function () {
    for (var b = 0;
 b < game.animationArray.length;
 b++) {
      var c = game.animationArray[b],
        a = c.animationArray[c.currentFrame],
        d = {},
        g = null,
        g = c.reverseSide
          ? game.fightInfo.getAttacker().wears
          : game.fightInfo.getDefender().wears;

      if (game.moveSet != void 0)
        ((d.x =
          game.moveSet.dir == true
            ? -1 * this.vector.x[this.vector.x.length - 1]
            : this.vector.x[this.vector.x.length - 1]),
          (d.y = 0));

      c.drawer(a, this, this.rubbish, g, d);

      c.drawEffect(this, this.rubbish, d);

      c.callBack();

    }
  };

  a.prototype.drawBlood = function () {
    this.bloodLeftText = null;

    this.bloodLeftText = new Quark.Text(
      game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp +
        "/" +
        game.fightInfo.getAttacker().maxHp,
      { fontSize: 34, color: "#ffffff", fontWeight: "bold" },
    );

    this.bloodLeftText.x = 260;

    this.bloodLeftText.y = 77;

    if (game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp > 0)
      ((this.bloodLeft = BitmapFactory.createBitmap({
        id: "xuetiao_l",
        src: "resource_12",
        rect: [
          659,
          632,
          Math.round(
            (357 * game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp) /
              game.fightInfo.getAttacker().maxHp,
          ),
          33,
        ],
      })),
        (this.bloodLeft.x = 152),
        (this.bloodLeft.y = 77),
        this.addChild(this.bloodLeft),
        this.bgRubbish.push(this.bloodLeft));

    this.addChild(this.bloodLeftText);

    this.bgRubbish.push(this.bloodLeftText);

    this.bloodRightText = null;

    this.bloodRightText = new Quark.Text(
      game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp +
        "/" +
        game.fightInfo.getDefender().maxHp,
      { fontSize: 34, color: "#ffffff", fontWeight: "bold" },
    );

    this.bloodRightText.x = 795;

    this.bloodRightText.y = 77;

    if (game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp > 0) {
      var b = Math.round(
          659 +
            357 *
              (1 -
                game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp /
                  game.fightInfo.getDefender().maxHp),
        ),
        c = Math.round(
          (357 * game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp) /
            game.fightInfo.getDefender().maxHp,
        );

      this.bloodRight = BitmapFactory.createBitmap({
        id: "xuetiao_l",
        src: "resource_12",
        rect: [b, 632, c, 33],
      });

      this.bloodRight.x = 1016 - c;

      this.bloodRight.y = 77;

      this.addChild(this.bloodRight);

      this.bgRubbish.push(this.bloodRight);

    }
    this.addChild(this.bloodRightText);

    this.bgRubbish.push(this.bloodRightText);

  };

  a.prototype.fightingProp = function () {
    if (
      !game.fightInfo.animationEnd &&
      !this.isSkip &&
      game.fightInfo.resultView != true &&
      (game.fightInfo.getType() == 0 ||
        game.fightInfo.getType() == 1 ||
        game.fightInfo.getType() == 3 ||
        game.fightInfo.getType() == 5 ||
        game.fightInfo.getType() == 4 ||
        game.fightInfo.getType() == 7) &&
      game.fightInfo.getFightingReward() != void 0 &&
      game.fightInfo.getFightingReward() != null
    )
      if ((this.fightingTimes++, this.fightingTimes == 340 + this.counter)) {
        var b = new Quark.FightProps({
          id: game.fightInfo.getFightingReward()[2].id,
          num: game.fightInfo.getFightingReward()[2].num,
          index: 2,
        });

        stage.addChild(b);

      } else
        this.fightingTimes == 180 + this.counter
          ? ((b = new Quark.FightProps({
              id: game.fightInfo.getFightingReward()[1].id,
              num: game.fightInfo.getFightingReward()[1].num,
              index: 1,
            })),
            stage.addChild(b))
          : this.fightingTimes == 30 + this.counter &&
            ((b = new Quark.FightProps({
              id: game.fightInfo.getFightingReward()[0].id,
              num: game.fightInfo.getFightingReward()[0].num,
              index: 0,
            })),
            stage.addChild(b));

  };

  a.prototype.isEnd = function () {
    if (
      game.fightInfo.animationEnd &&
      ((this.isStart = false), this.removeTitle(), this.isAlertEndWindow)
    )
      if (((this.isAlertEndWindow = false), game.fightInfo.getType() == 0))
        game.state = STATE.BATTLERESULT;

      else if (game.fightInfo.getType() == 1) game.state = STATE.BATTLERESULT;

      else if (game.fightInfo.getType() == 2)
        if (game.rankFight == void 0) game.state = STATE.BATTLERESULT;

        else if (
          game.rankFight.getCurrentRound() < game.rankFight.getTotalRound()
        ) {
          var b = this,
            c;

          game.alert("\u7ee7\u7eed\u89c2\u770b\u4e0b\u573a", function () {
            var c = Number(game.rankFight.getCurrentRound()) + Number(1);

            game.rankFight.setCurrentRound(c);

            game.fightInfo.animationEnd = false;

            game.fightInfo = null;

            game.fightInfo = new FightInfo(2);

            c == 2
              ? game.fightInfo.setFightData(game.rankFight.getCombat2())
              : game.fightInfo.setFightData(game.rankFight.getCombat3());

            b.index = -1;

            b.rest = new Rest(true);

            b.rest.currentFrame = 0;

            game.animationArray[0] = b.rest;

            b.rest1 = new Rest(false);

            b.rest1.currentFrame = 0;

            game.animationArray[1] = b.rest1;

            b.go();

          });

        } else game.state = STATE.BATTLERESULT;

      else if (game.fightInfo.getType() == 3)
        game.arena.getCurrentRound() == 1
          ? ((b = this),
            b.removeTitle(),
            b.alert1(game.arena.getTips(), function () {
              game.arena.setCurrentRound(2);

              game.fightInfo.animationEnd = false;

                game.arena.getSecondfightingProp(),
              );

                game.showWaiting(false);

                game.loadArr = [];

                b.removeAlert();

                b.startAttHp = game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp;

                b.startDefHp = game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp;

                b.index = -1;

                b.rest = new Rest(true);

                b.rest.currentFrame = 0;

                game.animationArray[0] = b.rest;

                b.rest1 = new Rest(false);

                b.rest1.currentFrame = 0;

                game.animationArray[1] = b.rest1;

                game.moveSet = game.current_FightSet.MapData;

                this.vector = { x: [], y: [] };

                for (var c in game.current_FightSet.MapData_ini.x)
                  ((this.vector.x[c] = game.current_FightSet.MapData_ini.x[c]),
                    (this.vector.y[c] =
                      game.current_FightSet.MapData_ini.y[c]));

                b.go();

              });

            }))
          : ((b = this),

              b.removeAlert();

              game.player.getBase().exp >= game.player.getBase().nextExp &&

                    game.state = STATE.BATTLERESULT;

                  })
                : (game.state = STATE.ARENA);

            }));

      else if (game.fightInfo.getType() == 4 || game.fightInfo.getType() == 7)
        game.rankFight.getCurrentRound() < game.rankFight.getTotalRound()

                          b.talk = 0;

                          game.moveSet = game.current_FightSet.MapData;

                          this.vector = { x: [], y: [] };

                          for (var a in game.current_FightSet.MapData_ini.x)
                            ((this.vector.x[a] =
                              game.current_FightSet.MapData_ini.x[a]),
                              (this.vector.y[a] =
                                game.current_FightSet.MapData_ini.y[a]));

                          game.fightInfo.animationEnd = false;

                          b.removeAlert();

                          b.go();

                        } else game.alert(c.msg);

                      },
                    );

                  } else
                    JsonLoader.quitStage(
                      game.npcFight.getStageId(),
                      function (c) {
                        c == true
                          ? (b.removeAlert(),
                            (game.player.stages.getValue(
                              game.npcFight.getStageId(),
                            ).keep = false),
                            (game.player.stages.getValue(
                              game.npcFight.getStageId(),
                            ).revive = false),
                            (game.player.stages.getValue(
                              game.npcFight.getStageId(),
                            ).npcIndex = 1),
                            game.player.levelRewards != null &&
                            game.player.levelRewards != void 0
                              ? (game.fightInfo.setType(8),
                                (game.state = STATE.BATTLERESULT))
                              : (game.state = STATE.MISSION))
                          : game.alert(c.msg, function () {
                              game.state = STATE.MISSION;

                            });

                      },
                    );

                },
                true,
              ));

  };

  a.prototype.accountLastBlood = function () {
    var b = game.fightInfo.getCombatLog();

    if (this.index < b.length - 1) {
      game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp = this.startAttHp;

      game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp = this.startDefHp;

      for (var c = 0;
 c < b.length - 1;
 c++) {
        var a = b[c];

        if (a.attackHurt > 0)
          a.attackPlayer == 0
            ? (game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp =
                game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp - a.attackHurt)
            : (game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp =
                game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp - a.attackHurt);

        if (a.attackAddBlood > 0)
          a.attackPlayer == 0
            ? (game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp =
                Number(game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp) +
                Number(a.attackAddBlood))
            : (game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp =
                Number(game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp) +
                Number(a.attackAddBlood));

        if (a.reboundHurt > 0)
          a.attackPlayer == 0
            ? (game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp =
                game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp - a.reboundHurt)
            : (game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp =
                game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp - a.reboundHurt);

        if (a.backHurt > 0)
          a.attackPlayer == 0
            ? (game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp =
                game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp - a.backHurt)
            : (game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp =
                game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp - a.backHurt);

        if (a.continueHurt > 0)
          a.attackPlayer == 0
            ? (game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp =
                game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp - a.continueHurt)
            : (game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp =
                game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp - a.continueHurt);

        if (a.weaponHurt > 0)
          a.attackPlayer == 0
            ? (game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp =
                game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp - a.weaponHurt)
            : (game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp =
                game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp - a.weaponHurt);

      }
      if (game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp < 0)
        game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp = 0;

      if (game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp < 0)
        game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp = 0;

    }
  };

  a.prototype.viewBlood = function () {
    if (game.fightInfo.defendBloodView == true) {
      var b = false,
        c = 1;

      game.fightInfo.defendBloodView = false;

      if (game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].attackHurt > 0) {
        if (game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].isDouble == 1)
          this.startShake = true;

        if (
          game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].attackType == 3 &&
          game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].attackMethedId == 23
        )
          var b = true,
            c = Number(game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].multiple),
            a = Math.round(
              Number(game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].attackHurt) / c,
            ),
            d = Math.round(
              Number(game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].attackHurt),
            );

        if (b) {
          for (b = 0;
 b < c;
 b++) {
            b == c - 1 && (a = d);

            var g = new Q.BitmapText({
              text: "-" + a,
              src: "fightNum_r",
              isDouble: game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].isDouble,
              gap: 0,
              autoSize: true,
              animationData: this.bloodStyle,
            });

            if (game.animationArray[0].reverseSide) {
              game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp =
                game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp - a;

              d -= a;

              if (game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp < 0)
                game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp = 0;

              var i = Math.round(Math.random() * 100),
                j = Math.random() * 10 > 5 ? 1 : -1;

              i *= j;

              g.y = 270 + i;

              g.x = 300 + i;

            } else {
              game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp =
                game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp - a;

              d -= a;

              if (game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp < 0)
                game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp = 0;

              i = Math.round(Math.random() * 100);

              j = Math.random() * 10 > 5 ? 1 : -1;

              i *= j;

              g.y = 270 + i;

              g.x = 936 + i;

            }
            stage.addChild(g);

          }
          this.drawBlood();

        } else {
          g = new Q.BitmapText({
            text: "-" + game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].attackHurt,
            src: "fightNum_r",
            isDouble: game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].isDouble,
            gap: 0,
            autoSize: true,
            animationData: this.bloodStyle,
          });

          if (game.animationArray[0].reverseSide) {
            game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp =
              game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp -
              game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].attackHurt;

            if (game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp < 0)
              game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp = 0;

            g.y = 270;

            g.x = 300;

          } else {
            game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp =
              game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp -
              game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].attackHurt;

            if (game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp < 0)
              game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp = 0;

            g.y = 270;

            g.x = 936;

          }
          this.drawBlood();

          stage.addChild(g);

        }
      } else if (
        game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].attackHurt == 0 &&
        game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].defendType == 2 &&
        game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].defendMethedId == 16
      ) {
        g = new Q.BitmapText({
          text: "-" + game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].attackHurt,
          src: "fightNum_r",
          isDouble: game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].isDouble,
          gap: 0,
          autoSize: true,
          animationData: this.bloodStyle,
        });

        if (game.animationArray[0].reverseSide) {
          game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp =
            game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp -
            game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].attackHurt;

          if (game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp < 0)
            game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp = 0;

          g.y = 270;

          g.x = 300;

        } else {
          game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp =
            game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp -
            game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].attackHurt;

          if (game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp < 0)
            game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp = 0;

          g.y = 270;

          g.x = 936;

        }
        this.drawBlood();

        stage.addChild(g);

      }
    }
    if (game.fightInfo.weaponHurt) {
      if (game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].isDouble == 1)
        this.startShake = true;

      game.fightInfo.weaponHurt = false;

      if (game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].weaponHurt > 0) {
        g = new Q.BitmapText({
          text: "-" + game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].weaponHurt,
          src: "fightNum_r",
          gap: 0,
          autoSize: true,
          animationData: this.bloodStyle,
        });

        if (game.animationArray[0].reverseSide) {
          game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp =
            game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp -
            game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].weaponHurt;

          if (game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp < 0)
            game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp = 0;

          g.y = 270;

          g.x = 1050;

        } else {
          game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp =
            game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp -
            game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].weaponHurt;

          if (game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp < 0)
            game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp = 0;

          g.y = 270;

          g.x = 150;

        }
        this.drawBlood();

        stage.addChild(g);

      }
    }
    if (
      game.fightInfo.attackBloodView &&

      game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].attackAddBlood > 0)
    )
      ((g = new Q.BitmapText({
        text: "+" + game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].attackAddBlood,
        src: "fightNum_g",
        gap: 0,
        autoSize: true,
        animationData: this.bloodStyle,
      })),
        game.animationArray[1].reverseSide
          ? ((game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp =
              Number(game.fightInfo.getAttacker().headHp) +
              Number(game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].attackAddBlood)),
            (g.y = 270),
            (g.x = 150))
          : ((game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp =
              Number(game.fightInfo.getDefender().headHp) +
              Number(game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].attackAddBlood)),

        this.drawBlood(),

    if (game.fightInfo.beatBackBloodView) {
      if (game.fightInfo.getCombatLog()[this.index].isDouble == 1)
        this.startShake = true;

      game.fightInfo.beatBackBloodView = false;

  };

  a.prototype.fightNextRound = function () {
    if (game.fightInfo != null) {
      this.index++;

      var b = game.fightInfo.getCombatLogByIndex(this.index);

      game.fightController.getRoundAnimation(b, this.index);

      game.animationArray = null;

      game.animationArray = [];

      game.fightRound.Attacker == null
        ? (game.animationArray.push(game.fightRound.Defender),
          game.animationArray.push(game.fightRound.Rester))
        : (game.animationArray.push(game.fightRound.Rester),
          game.animationArray.push(game.fightRound.Attacker));

    }
  };

  a.prototype.skipFight = function () {
    var b = game.fightInfo.getType();

    b == 4 || b == 7
      ? ((b = game.rankFight.getIsWin()), (b = b == 1 ? 1 : 0))
      : ((b = game.fightInfo.getWinner()), (b = b == 0 ? 1 : 0));

    if (game.fightInfo != null)
      (this.index++,
        game.fightController.getRoundAnimation(
          {
            attackPlayer: b,
            attackType: "-1",
            attackMethedId: "0",
            attackHurt: "0",
            defendType: "0",
            defendMethedId: "0",
            backHurt: "0",
            reboundHurt: "0",
            continueHurt: "0",
            attackAddBlood: "0",
            adefendAddBlood: "0",
            level: "0",
            weaponHurt: "0",
            effect: "0",
            multiple: "0",
            isDouble: "0",
          },
          this.index,
        ),
        (game.animationArray = null),
        (game.animationArray = []),
        game.fightRound.Attacker == null
          ? (game.animationArray.push(game.fightRound.Defender),
            game.animationArray.push(game.fightRound.Rester))
          : (game.animationArray.push(game.fightRound.Rester),
            game.animationArray.push(game.fightRound.Attacker)));

  };

  a.prototype.go = function () {
    game.loadRes(game.loadArr2, function () {
      game.loadArr2 = [];

    });

    this.counter = Math.round(Math.random() * 130);

    if (this.resArr.length > 0) {
      for (var b in this.resArr)
        this.contains(this.resArr[b]) && this.removeChild(this.resArr[b]);

      this.resArr = [];

    }
    this.fightingTimes = 0;

    this.isAlertEndWindow = true;

    this.isSkip = false;

    timer.addListener(Q.Tween);

    b = BitmapFactory.getImage("go");

    this.bitmap = BitmapFactory.createBitmap({
      id: "",
      imageData: { image: b, rect: [542, 28, 198, 179] },
    });

    this.bitmap.regX = this.bitmap.width / 2;

    this.bitmap.regY = this.bitmap.height / 2;

    this.bitmap.x = 600;

    this.bitmap.y = 300;

    this.bitmap.scaleX = 1;

    this.bitmap.scaleY = 1;

    this.bitmap.alpha = 1;

    this.addChild(this.bitmap);

    this.bgRubbish.push(this.bitmap);

    this.tween_3(this.bitmap, [756, 25, 195, 185]);

  };

  a.prototype.tween_3 = function (b, c) {
    var a = this;

    Q.Tween.to(
      b,
      { scaleX: 1.2, scaleY: 1.2, alpha: 1 },
      {
        time: 250,
        onComplete: function () {
          Q.Tween.to(
            b,
            { scaleX: 1, scaleY: 1, alpha: 1 },
            {
              time: 250,
              onComplete: function () {
                b.setRect(c);

                b.regX = b.width / 2;

                b.regY = b.height / 2;

                a.tween_2(b, [982, 25, 90, 183]);

              },
            },
          );

        },
      },
    );

  };

  a.prototype.tween_2 = function (b, c) {
    var a = this;

    Q.Tween.to(
      b,
      { scaleX: 1.2, scaleY: 1.2, alpha: 1 },
      {
        time: 250,
        onComplete: function () {
          Q.Tween.to(
            b,
            { scaleX: 1, scaleY: 1, alpha: 1 },
            {
              time: 250,
              onComplete: function () {
                b.setRect(c);

                b.regX = b.width / 2;

                b.regY = b.height / 2;

                a.tween_1(b, [5, 3, 495, 215]);

              },
            },
          );

        },
      },
    );

  };

  a.prototype.tween_1 = function (b, c) {
    var a = this;

    Q.Tween.to(
      b,
      { scaleX: 1.2, scaleY: 1.2, alpha: 1 },
      {
        time: 250,
        onComplete: function () {
          Q.Tween.to(
            b,
            { scaleX: 1, scaleY: 1, alpha: 1 },
            {
              time: 250,
              onComplete: function () {
                b.setRect(c);

                b.regX = b.width / 2;

                b.regY = b.height / 2;

                a.tween_go(b);

              },
            },
          );

        },
      },
    );

  };

  a.prototype.tween_go = function (b) {
    var c = this;

    Q.Tween.to(
      b,
      { scaleX: 1.2, scaleY: 1.2, alpha: 1 },
      {
        time: 350,
        onComplete: function () {
          Q.Tween.to(
            b,
            { scaleX: 1, scaleY: 1, alpha: 1 },
            {
              time: 350,
              onComplete: function () {
                timer.removeListener(Q.Tween);

                c.fightNextRound();

                c.isStart = true;

              },
            },
          );

        },
      },
    );

  };

  a.prototype.getStyle = function () {
    return [
      { alpha: 1, scaleX: 1, scaleY: 1 },
      { alpha: 1, scaleX: 1.3, scaleY: 1.3 },
      { alpha: 1, scaleX: 1.4, scaleY: 1.4 },
      { alpha: 1, scaleX: 1.6, scaleY: 1.6 },

    ];

  a.prototype.resultTitle = function () {};

  a.prototype.drawSkipButton = function () {
    var b = this,
      c = false;

    game.player.isVIP == true
      ? (c = true)
      : game.player.getBase().level > 9 && (c = true);

    if (game.fightInfo.getType() == 5) {
      var a = game.npcFight.getStageId(),
        a = game.player.stages.getValue(a);

      if (a == void 0 || a.passed == false) c = false;

    }
    if (c)
      ((c = BitmapFactory.createButton({
        id: "tiaoguo",
        x: 983,
        y: 609,
        src: "resource_4",
        label: "7",
      })),
        (a = BitmapFactory.createBitmap({
          id: "hop",
          x: 1029,
          y: 623,
          src: "resource_2",
          label: "44",
        })),
        (c.onPress = function () {
          if (b.index > -1 && !b.isSkip) {
            if (b.index == game.fightInfo.getCombatLog().length - 3)
              b.isSkip = true;

            game.player.isVIP
              ? game.player.getBase().level < 10
                ? game.showTips(
                    "\u4f60\u7684\u7b49\u7ea7\u4e0d\u591f10\u7ea7,\u4e0d\u80fd\u8df3\u8fc7\u3002",
                    2,
                  )
                : ((b.isSkip = true), b.removeChildById("tiaoguo"), b.skip())
              : game.showTips(
                  "\u4f60\u8fd8\u4e0d\u662f\u8d85\u7ea7\u677e\u9f20,\u4e0d\u80fd\u8df3\u8fc7\u3002",
                  2,
                );

          }
        }),
        this.addChild(c),
        this.addChild(a),
        this.rubbish.push(c),
        this.rubbish.push(a));

  };

  a.prototype.tween_3 = function (b, a) {

            {
              time: 250,
              onComplete: function () {

  b.prototype.clearRubbish = function () {
    for (var b = 0;
 b < this.rubbish.length;
 b++)
      (this.removeChild(this.rubbish[b]), (this.rubbish[b] = null));

    for (b = 0;
 b < this.rubbishUI.length;
 b++)
      (this.removeChild(this.rubbishUI[b]), (this.rubbishUI[b] = null));

    this.rubbish = null;

    this.rubbish = [];

    this.rubbishUI = null;

    this.rubbishUI = [];

  };

  b.prototype.drawUI = function () {
    for (var b = this.getChildren("main"), a = 1;
 a < b.length;
 a++)
      (this.addChild(b[a]), this.rubbishUI.push(b[a]));

  };

  b.prototype.update = function () {
    if (this.squWalk instanceof SquWalk) {
      this.clearRubbish();

      this.drawDisciple();

      var b = this.squWalk.animationArray[this.squWalk.currentFrame],
        a = game.player.getWears();

      Drawable.drawEffect(this.effectData2[j], this, this.rubbish, this.cache2);

      this.squWalk.drawer(b, this, this.rubbish, a);

      this.squWalk.drawEffect(this, this.rubbish);

      this.squWalk.callBack();

      Drawable.drawEffect(this.effectData1[i], this, this.rubbish, this.cache1);

      i >= this.effectData1.length - 1 ? (i = 0) : i++;

      j >= this.effectData2.length - 1 ? (j = 0) : j++;

      if (game.player.loginData.levelUp === true)
        ((game.state = STATE.MAINLEVELUP),
          (game.player.loginData.levelUp = false));

      game.player.loginData.vipData.vip == false &&
        (c.contains(this.cjss) && c.removeChild(this.cjss),
        c.contains(this.text_cjss) && c.removeChild(this.text_cjss));

      b = parseInt(
        g[2] *
          Math.min(
            game.player.getBase().energy / game.player.getBase().maxEnergy,
            1,
          ),
        10,
      );

      b = Math.max(1, b);

      this.tilitiao.rectWidth = b;

      this.tili.setText(
        game.player.getBase().energy + "/" + game.player.getBase().maxEnergy,
      );

      game.player.newbie < 16 &&
        f != void 0 &&
        (f != void 0 &&
          f.y == 400 &&
          Q.Tween.to(
            f,
            { y: 490 },
            {
              time: 300,
              onComplete: function () {
                Q.Tween.to(
                  f,
                  { y: 400 },
                  { time: 300, onComplete: function () {} },
                );

              },
            },
          ),
        f != void 0 &&
          f.y == 120 &&
          Q.Tween.to(
            f,
            { y: 160 },
            {
              time: 300,
              onComplete: function () {
                Q.Tween.to(
                  f,
                  { y: 120 },
                  { time: 300, onComplete: function () {} },
                );

              },
            },
          ));

      if (
        (game.player.generalNew == true ||
          game.player.rankNew == true ||
          game.player.requestNew == true ||
          game.player.msgBoxNew == true) &&
        game.player.newbie > 1 &&
        e != void 0 &&
        e.visible == false
      )
        e.visible = true;

      game.player.newbie > 11 &&
        game.player.activity_lottery &&
        (u < 45 ? u++ : ((u = 0), d()));

      this.drawUI();

    }
  };

    b =
      a == "weapon"
        ? new MyWeapon({
            id: "weapon",

            height: stage.height,
          })
        : a == "skill"
          ? new Skill({
              id: "skill",
              person: d.person,
              relation: d.relation,

              height: stage.height,

              relation: d.relation,
              woodmanRecord: d.woodmanRecord,
              data: d.data,
              x: 0,
              y: 0,
              width: stage.width,
              height: stage.height,
            });

    stage.addChild(b);

  };

var Animation = function (a, d) {
  this.data = AnimationData.getAnimation(a, d);

  this.animationArray = this.data.animationArray;

  this.currentFrame = this.data.currentFrame;

  this.totalFrame = this.data.totalFrame;

  this.type = this.data.type;

  this.weaponId = this.data.weaponId;

  this.id = this.data.id;

  this.isOver = this.data.isOver;

};

Animation.prototype.callBack = function () {};

Animation.prototype.drawEffect = function () {};

Animation.prototype.drawer = function () {};

var AnimationData = {
  getAnimation: function (a, d) {
    var b = {},
      c;

    if (a == -1) c = AnimationStr.mainWalk1;

    else if (a == -4) c = AnimationStr.mainWalk2;

    else if (a == -3) c = AnimationStr.mainWalk3;

    else if (a == -2) c = AnimationStr.animationStart;

    else if (a == 0) c = AnimationStr.fightCommonAttack;

    else if (a == 1) c = AnimationStr.fightWeaponAttack;

    else if (a == 2) c = AnimationStr.fightThrowWeaponAttack;

    else if (a == 3) c = AnimationStr.bjBack;

    else if (a == 4) c = AnimationStr.fanhui;

    else if (a == 5) c = AnimationStr.beatBack;

    else if (a == 6) c = AnimationStr.runAround;

    else if (a == 7) c = AnimationStr.hitMe;

    else if (a == 8) c = AnimationStr.die;

  parseData: function (a, d) {
    for (
      var b = "label,alpha,a,b,c,d,tx,ty".split(","),
        c = a.split(":"),
        e = [],
        f = 0;

      f < c.length;

      f++
    ) {
      for (var g = [], i = c[f].split("|"), j = 0;
 j < i.length;
 j++) {
        var h = i[j].split(","),
          n = {};

        n.label = h[0];

        for (var s = 1;
 s < h.length;
 s++) n[b[s]] = Number(h[s]);

        parseInt(n.label) > 1e3 && (n.label = d);

        n.label += "";

        g.push(n);

      }
      e.push(g);

    }
    return e;

  },
  parseNPCData: function (a) {
    for (
      var d = "label,alpha,a,b,c,d,tx,ty".split(","),
        a = a.split(":"),
        b = [],
        c = 0;

      c < a.length;

      c++
    ) {
      for (var e = [], f = a[c].split("|"), g = 0;
 g < f.length;
 g++) {
        var i = f[g].split(","),
          j = {};

        j.label = i[0];

        for (var h = 1;
 h < i.length;
 h++) j[d[h]] = Number(i[h]);

        j.label += "";

        e.push(j);

      }
      b.push(e);

    }
    return b;

  },
  getNpc: function (a) {
    var d = {},
      b;

    if (a == 0) b = AnimationStr.tl_common_attack;

    var c = b.split(":"),
      e = [],

    d.animationArray = e;

  getNpcObject: function (a, d, b, c, e, f, g) {
    var i = {};

    i.id = a;

    i.x = d;

    i.y = b;

    i.rotation = c;

    i.alpha = e;

    i.scaleX = f;

    i.scaleY = g;

    return i;

  },
  getAnimationObject: function (a, d, b, c, e, f) {
    var g = {};

    g.f = a;

    g.n = d;

    g.x = Math.round(b);

    g.y = Math.round(c);

    g.a = Math.round(e);

    g.index = f;

    return g;

  },
  p: function (a, d) {
    var b = {};

    b.x = Math.round(a);

    b.y = Math.round(d);

    return b;

  },
};

EffectData = {
  getEffectData: function (a) {
    for (var a = a.split(":"), d = [], b = 0;
 b < a.length;
 b++) {
      for (var c = a[b].split("|"), e = [], f = 0;
 f < c.length;
 f++) {
        var g = c[f].split(","),
          g = this.getTilesGameInfo(g[0], g[1], g[2], g[3], g[4], g[5], g[6]);

        e.push(g);

      }
      d.push(e);

    }
    return d;

  },
  getTilesGameInfo: function (a, d, b, c, e, f, g) {
    var i = {};

    i.id = Number(a);

    i.x = Math.round(Number(d));

    i.y = Math.round(Number(b));

    i.r = Math.round(Number(c));

    i.a = Math.round(Number(e));

    i.sx = f;

    i.sy = g;

    return i;

  },
};

var Npc = function (a) {
  this.data = AnimationData.getNpc(a);

  this.animationArray = this.data.animationArray;

  this.currentFrame = this.data.currentFrame;

  this.totalFrame = this.data.totalFrame;

  this.type = this.data.type;

};

Npc.prototype.callBack = function () {};

Npc.prototype.drawEffect = function () {};

Npc.prototype.drawer = function () {};

var Round = function () {
    this.isDouble =
      this.effect =
      this.weaponHurt =
      this.level =
      this.adefendAddBlood =
      this.attackAddBlood =
      this.continueHurt =
      this.reboundHurt =
      this.backHurt =
      this.defendMethedId =
      this.defendType =
      this.attackHurt =
      this.attackMethedId =
      this.attackType =
      this.attackPlayer =
        0;

  },
  RoundAnimation = function () {
    this.Winner =
      this.Rester =
      this.RunBacker =
      this.HurtRunBacker =
      this.BeatBacker =
      this.Defender =
      this.Attacker =
        null;

    this.isOver = false;

  };

RoundAnimation.prototype.buildAttacker = function (a, d) {
  var b = true,
    b = a.attackPlayer == 0 ? true : false;

  if (d && a.attackPlayer != game.fightInfo.getWinner() && a.reboundHurt < 1)
    this.Defender =
      game.fightInfo.getWinner() == 0 ? new Die(false) : new Die(true);

  else if (a.attackType != -1)
    if (a.attackType == 0) this.Attacker = new Think(b);

    else if (a.attackType == 1) this.Attacker = new CommonAttack(b);

    else if (a.attackType == 2)
      this.Attacker =
        weaponsMap.getValue(a.attackMethedId).type == "\u8fd1\u6218"
          ? new WeaponAttack(b, a.attackMethedId, a.level)
          : new ThrowWeaponAttack(b, a.attackMethedId, a.level);

    else if (a.attackType == 3)
      if (a.attackMethedId == 8) this.Attacker = new Skill_8(b);

      else if (a.attackMethedId == 12) this.Attacker = new Skill_9(b);

      else if (a.attackMethedId == 13) this.Attacker = new Skill_13(b);

      else if (a.attackMethedId == 14) this.Attacker = new Skill_14(b);

      else if (a.attackMethedId == 15) this.Attacker = new Skill_15(b);

      else if (a.attackMethedId == 17) this.Attacker = new Skill_17(b);

      else if (a.attackMethedId == 18) this.Attacker = new Skill_18(b);

      else if (a.attackMethedId == 23)
        this.Attacker = new Skill_23(b, a.multiple);

};

RoundAnimation.prototype.buildDefender = function (a, d) {
  var b = true,
    b = a.attackPlayer == 0 ? true : false;

  if (d && a.attackPlayer == game.fightInfo.getWinner() && a.reboundHurt < 1)
    this.Defender = new Die(!b);

  else if (a.defendType == 0) {
    if (a.attackType != -1)
      if (a.attackType == 1) this.Defender = new HitMe(!b);

      else if (a.attackType == 2) this.Defender = new HitMe(!b);

      else if (a.attackType == 3)
        this.Defender =
          a.attackMethedId == 8
            ? new Skill_8_defend(!b)
            : a.attackMethedId == 12
              ? new Skill_9_defend(!b)
              : a.attackMethedId == 15
                ? new Skill_15_defend(!b)
                : a.attackMethedId == 18
                  ? a.effect == 0
                    ? new Skill_18_defend_1(!b)
                    : new Skill_18_defend_2(!b)
                  : new HitMe(!b);

  } else if (a.defendType == 1) this.Defender = new RunAround(!b);

  else if (a.defendType == 2)
    if (a.defendMethedId == 6) this.Defender = new Skill_6(!b);

    else if (a.defendMethedId == 7) this.Defender = new Skill_7(!b);

    else if (a.defendMethedId == 16) this.Defender = new Skill_16(!b);

};

RoundAnimation.prototype.buildBeatBacker = function (a) {
  var d = true,
    d = a.attackPlayer == 0 ? true : false;

  if (a.reboundHurt > 0) this.BeatBacker = new BeatBack(!d);

};

RoundAnimation.prototype.buildHurtRunBacker = function (a, d) {
  var b = true,
    b = a.attackPlayer == 0 ? true : false;

  if (d) this.HurtRunBacker = new HurtBackDie(b);

  else if (a.reboundHurt > 0) this.HurtRunBacker = new HurtRunBack(b);

};

RoundAnimation.prototype.buildRunBacker = function (a) {
  var d = true,
    d = a.attackPlayer == 0 ? true : false;

  if (a.attackType == 1) this.RunBacker = new RunBack(d);

  else if (
    a.attackType == 2 &&
    weaponsMap.getValue(a.attackMethedId).type == "\u8fd1\u6218"
  )
    this.RunBacker = new RunBack(d);

};

RoundAnimation.prototype.buildRester = function (a) {
  this.Rester = a.attackPlayer == 0 ? new Rest(false) : new Rest(true);

};

RoundAnimation.prototype.buildWinner = function (a, d) {
  if (d)
    this.Winner =
      game.fightInfo.getWinner() == 0 ? new Win(true) : new Win(false);

};

RoundAnimation.prototype.buildAnimation = function (a, d) {
  this.buildAttacker(a, d);

  this.buildDefender(a, d);

  this.buildBeatBacker(a, d);

  this.buildHurtRunBacker(a, d);

  this.buildRunBacker(a, d);

  this.buildRester(a, d);

  this.buildWinner(a, d);

};

window.RoundAnimation = RoundAnimation;

var RoundAnimationNPC = function () {
  this.Winner =
    this.Rester =
    this.RunBacker =
    this.HurtRunBacker =
    this.BeatBacker =
    this.Defender =
    this.Attacker =
      null;

  this.isOver = false;

};

RoundAnimationNPC.prototype.buildAttacker = function (a, d) {
  if (a.attackPlayer == 0)
    if (d && a.attackPlayer != game.fightInfo.getWinner() && a.reboundHurt < 1)
      this.Defender =
        game.fightInfo.getWinner() == 0 ? new Die(false) : new Die(true);

    else {
      if (a.attackType != -1)
        if (a.attackType == 0) this.Attacker = new Think(true);

        else if (a.attackType == 1) this.Attacker = new CommonAttack(true);

        else if (a.attackType == 2)
          this.Attacker =
            weaponsMap.getValue(a.attackMethedId).type == "\u8fd1\u6218"
              ? new WeaponAttack(true, a.attackMethedId, a.level)
              : new ThrowWeaponAttack(true, a.attackMethedId, a.level);

        else if (a.attackType == 3)
          if (a.attackMethedId == 8) this.Attacker = new Skill_8(true);

          else if (a.attackMethedId == 12) this.Attacker = new Skill_9(true);

          else if (a.attackMethedId == 13) this.Attacker = new Skill_13(true);

          else if (a.attackMethedId == 14) this.Attacker = new Skill_14(true);

          else if (a.attackMethedId == 15) this.Attacker = new Skill_15(true);

          else if (a.attackMethedId == 17) this.Attacker = new Skill_17(true);

          else if (a.attackMethedId == 18) this.Attacker = new Skill_18(true);

          else if (a.attackMethedId == 23)
            this.Attacker = new Skill_23(true, a.multiple);

    }
  else {
    var b = parseInt(game.fightInfo.getDefender().playerId);

    if (b < 118 && b > 99)
      if (
        d &&
        a.attackPlayer != game.fightInfo.getWinner() &&
        a.reboundHurt < 1
      )
        this.Defender = new tl_die();

      else if (a.attackType == 1) this.Attacker = new tl_common_attack();

      else {
        if (a.attackType == 3) this.Attacker = new tl_skill_attack();

      }
    else if (b < 136 && b >= 118)
      if (
        d &&
        a.attackPlayer != game.fightInfo.getWinner() &&
        a.reboundHurt < 1
      )
        this.Defender = new xh_die();

      else if (a.attackType == 3 && a.attackMethedId == 21)
        this.Attacker = new xh_common_attack();

      else {
        if (a.attackType == 3 && a.attackMethedId == 20)
          this.Attacker = new xh_skill_attack();

      }
    else if (b > 135 && b < 154)
      if (
        d &&
        a.attackPlayer != game.fightInfo.getWinner() &&
        a.reboundHurt < 1
      )
        this.Defender = new xm_die();

      else if (a.attackType == 1) this.Attacker = new xm_common_attack();

  }
};

RoundAnimationNPC.prototype.buildDefender = function (a, d) {
  var b = true;

  if (a.attackPlayer == 0)
    if (
      ((b = parseInt(game.fightInfo.getDefender().playerId)), b < 118 && b > 99)
    )
      if (
        d &&
        a.attackPlayer == game.fightInfo.getWinner() &&
        a.reboundHurt < 1
      )
        this.Defender = new tl_die();

      else {
        if (a.attackType != -1)
          if (a.defendType == 0) this.Defender = new tl_hitMe();

          else if (a.defendType == 1) this.Defender = new tl_runAround();

      }
    else if (b < 136 && b >= 118)
      if (
        d &&
        a.attackPlayer == game.fightInfo.getWinner() &&
        a.reboundHurt < 1
      )
        this.Defender = new xh_die();

      else {
        if (a.attackType != -1)
          if (a.defendType == 0) this.Defender = new xh_hitMe();

          else if (a.defendType == 1) this.Defender = new xh_runAround();

      }
    else if (b < 154 && b > 135)
      if (
        d &&
        a.attackPlayer == game.fightInfo.getWinner() &&
        a.reboundHurt < 1
      )
        this.Defender = new xm_die();

      else {
        if (a.attackType != -1)
          if (a.defendType == 0) this.Defender = new xm_hitMe();

          else if (a.defendType == 1) this.Defender = new xm_runAround();

          else if (a.defendType == 2) this.Defender = new xm_skill_attack();

      }
    else {
      if (b == 400 && a.defendType == 0 && a.attackType != -1)
        this.Defender =
          a.attackMethedId == 8 && a.attackType == 3
            ? new wood_8_hit()
            : a.attackMethedId == 12 && a.attackType == 3
              ? new wood_12_hit()
              : a.attackMethedId == 15 && a.attackType == 3
                ? new wood_15_hit()
                : new wood_hitMe();

    }
  else if (
    ((b = false),
    d && a.attackPlayer == game.fightInfo.getWinner() && a.reboundHurt < 1)
  )
    this.Defender = new Die(!b);

  else if (a.defendType == 0) {
    if (a.attackType != -1)
      if (a.attackType == 1) this.Defender = new HitMe(!b);

      else if (a.attackType == 2) this.Defender = new HitMe(!b);

      else if (a.attackType == 3) this.Defender = new HitMe(!b);

  } else if (a.defendType == 1) this.Defender = new RunAround(!b);

  else if (a.defendType == 2)
    if (a.defendMethedId == 6) this.Defender = new Skill_6(!b);

    else if (a.defendMethedId == 7) this.Defender = new Skill_7(!b);

    else if (a.defendMethedId == 16) this.Defender = new Skill_16(!b);

};

RoundAnimationNPC.prototype.buildBeatBacker = function (a) {
  var d = true;

  if (a.attackPlayer == 0) {
    if (a.reboundHurt > 0)
      if (
        ((a = parseInt(game.fightInfo.getDefender().playerId)),
        a < 118 && a > 99)
      )
        this.BeatBacker = new tl_beatBack();

      else if (a < 136 && a >= 118) this.BeatBacker = new xh_beatBack();

      else if (a < 154 && a > 135) this.BeatBacker = new xm_beatBack();

  } else if (a.reboundHurt > 0) this.BeatBacker = new BeatBack(true);

};

RoundAnimationNPC.prototype.buildHurtRunBacker = function (a, d) {
  if (a.attackPlayer == 0)
    if (d) this.HurtRunBacker = new HurtBackDie(true);

    else {
      if (a.reboundHurt > 0) this.HurtRunBacker = new HurtRunBack(true);

    }
  else {
    var b = parseInt(game.fightInfo.getDefender().playerId);

    if (b < 118 && b > 99)
      if (d) this.HurtRunBacker = new tl_hurtBackDie();

      else {
        if (a.reboundHurt > 0) this.HurtRunBacker = new tl_hurtRunBack();

      }
    else if (b < 154 && b > 135)
      if (d) this.HurtRunBacker = new xm_hurtBackDie();

      else if (a.reboundHurt > 0) this.HurtRunBacker = new xm_hurtRunBack();

  }
};

RoundAnimationNPC.prototype.buildRunBacker = function (a) {
  var d = true;

  if (a.attackPlayer == 0)
    if (((d = true), a.attackType == 1)) this.RunBacker = new RunBack(d);

    else {
      if (
        a.attackType == 2 &&
        weaponsMap.getValue(a.attackMethedId).type == "\u8fd1\u6218"
      )
        this.RunBacker = new RunBack(d);

    }
  else if (
    ((d = parseInt(game.fightInfo.getDefender().playerId)), d < 118 && d > 99)
  ) {
    if (a.attackType == 1) this.RunBacker = new tl_runBack();

  } else if (d < 154 && d > 135 && a.attackType == 1)
    this.RunBacker = new xm_runBack();

};

RoundAnimationNPC.prototype.buildRester = function (a) {
  if (a.attackPlayer == 0) {
    a = parseInt(game.fightInfo.getDefender().playerId);

    if (a < 118 && a > 99) this.Rester = new tl_rest(false);

    else if (a < 136 && a >= 118) this.Rester = new xh_rest(false);

    else if (a < 154 && a > 135) this.Rester = new xm_rest(false);

    if (a == 400) this.Rester = new wood_rest(false);

  } else this.Rester = new Rest(true);

};

RoundAnimationNPC.prototype.buildWinner = function (a, d) {
  if (d) {
    var b = parseInt(game.fightInfo.getDefender().playerId);

    if (game.fightInfo.getWinner() == 0) {
      if (b != 400) this.Winner = new Win(true);

    } else if (b < 118 && b > 99) this.Winner = new tl_win();

    else if (b < 136 && b >= 118) this.Winner = new xh_win();

    else if (b < 154 && b > 135) this.Winner = new xm_win();

  }
};

RoundAnimationNPC.prototype.buildAnimation = function (a, d) {
  this.buildAttacker(a, d);

  this.buildDefender(a, d);

  this.buildBeatBacker(a, d);

  this.buildHurtRunBacker(a, d);

  this.buildRunBacker(a, d);

  this.buildRester(a, d);

  this.buildWinner(a, d);

};

window.RoundAnimationNPC = RoundAnimationNPC;

var ThrowWeaponAttack = function (a, d, b) {
  this.reverseSide = a;

  this.weaponId = parseInt(d);

  this.level = parseInt(b);

  this.cache = this.SpecialEffect = this.effectSpeId = null;

  if (this.level > 10) {
    if (((this.weaponId += 1200), this.level > 12))
      this.effectSpeId = this.level - 10;

  } else if (this.level > 7) {
    if (((this.weaponId += 1100), this.level > 8))
      this.effectSpeId = this.level - 8;

  } else this.weaponId += 1e3;

  Animation.call(this, 2, this.weaponId);

  this.effectData = AnimationData.parseData(AnimationStr.effect_throw);

  if (this.effectSpeId != null)
    if (this.effectSpeId == 1) {
      if (game.lightning1Cache == void 0)
        game.lightning1Cache = new BitmapCache(["lightning1"], "all");

      this.cache = game.lightning1Cache;

      this.SpecialEffect = AnimationData.parseData(
        AnimationStr.throwweapon_lightning_1,
      );

    } else if (this.effectSpeId == 2) {
      if (game.lightning2Cache == void 0)
        game.lightning2Cache = new BitmapCache(["lightning2"], "all");

      this.cache = game.lightning2Cache;

      this.SpecialEffect = AnimationData.parseData(
        AnimationStr.throwweapon_lightning_2,
      );

    } else if (this.effectSpeId == 3) {
      if (game.lightning3Cache == void 0)
        game.lightning3Cache = new BitmapCache(["lightning3"], "all");

      this.cache = game.lightning3Cache;

      this.SpecialEffect = AnimationData.parseData(
        AnimationStr.throwweapon_lightning_3,
      );

    } else if (this.effectSpeId == 4) {
      if (game.lightning4Cache == void 0)
        game.lightning4Cache = new BitmapCache(["lightning4"], "all");

      this.cache = game.lightning4Cache;

      this.SpecialEffect = AnimationData.parseData(
        AnimationStr.throwweapon_lightning_4,
      );

    } else if (this.effectSpeId == 5) {
      if (game.lightning5Cache == void 0)
        game.lightning5Cache = new BitmapCache(["lightning5"], "all");

      this.cache = game.lightning5Cache;

      this.SpecialEffect = AnimationData.parseData(
        AnimationStr.throwweapon_lightning_5,
      );

    }
};

ThrowWeaponAttack.prototype.callBack = function () {
  this.currentFrame++;

  if (this.currentFrame == 2) game.fightInfo.continueHurt = true;

  if (this.currentFrame >= this.totalFrame)
    this.currentFrame = this.totalFrame - 1;

  if (this.currentFrame == 13)
    (game.fightController.notifying(StaticVars.getConstants("attackerMessage")),
      (game.fightInfo.attackBloodView = true));

};

ThrowWeaponAttack.prototype.drawer = function (a, d, b, c, e) {
  Drawable.drawSquirrel(
    a,
    d,
    b,
    c,
    this.reverseSide,
    this.currentFrame,
    this.weaponId,
    this.level,
    false,
    game.ssCache,
    this.reverseSide,
    e,
  );

};

ThrowWeaponAttack.prototype.drawEffect = function (a, d, b) {
  Drawable.drawEffect(
    this.effectData[this.currentFrame],
    a,
    d,
    game.throwCache,
    this.reverseSide,
    b,
  );

  this.SpecialEffect != void 0 &&
    Drawable.drawEffect(
      this.SpecialEffect[this.currentFrame],
      a,
      d,
      this.cache,
      this.reverseSide,
      b,
    );

};

var WeaponAttack = function (a, d, b) {
  this.reverseSide = a;

  this.weaponId = parseInt(d);

  this.level = parseInt(b);

  this.cache = this.SpecialEffect = this.effectSpeId = null;

  if (this.level > 10) {
    if (((this.weaponId += 1200), this.level > 12))
      this.effectSpeId = this.level - 10;

  } else if (this.level > 7) {
    if (((this.weaponId += 1100), this.level > 8))
      this.effectSpeId = this.level - 8;

  } else this.weaponId += 1e3;

  Animation.call(this, 1, this.weaponId);

  this.effectData = AnimationData.parseData(AnimationStr.effect_weapon);

  if (this.effectSpeId != null)
    if (this.effectSpeId == 1) {
      if (game.lightning1Cache == void 0)
        game.lightning1Cache = new BitmapCache(["lightning1"], "all");

      this.cache = game.lightning1Cache;

      this.SpecialEffect = AnimationData.parseData(
        AnimationStr.weapon_lightning_1,
      );

    } else if (this.effectSpeId == 2) {
      if (game.lightning2Cache == void 0)
        game.lightning2Cache = new BitmapCache(["lightning2"], "all");

      this.cache = game.lightning2Cache;

      this.SpecialEffect = AnimationData.parseData(
        AnimationStr.weapon_lightning_2,
      );

    } else if (this.effectSpeId == 3) {
      if (game.lightning3Cache == void 0)
        game.lightning3Cache = new BitmapCache(["lightning3"], "all");

      this.cache = game.lightning3Cache;

      this.SpecialEffect = AnimationData.parseData(
        AnimationStr.weapon_lightning_3,
      );

    } else if (this.effectSpeId == 4) {
      if (game.lightning4Cache == void 0)
        game.lightning4Cache = new BitmapCache(["lightning4"], "all");

      this.cache = game.lightning4Cache;

      this.SpecialEffect = AnimationData.parseData(
        AnimationStr.weapon_lightning_4,
      );

    } else if (this.effectSpeId == 5) {
      if (game.lightning5Cache == void 0)
        game.lightning5Cache = new BitmapCache(["lightning5"], "all");

      this.cache = game.lightning5Cache;

      this.SpecialEffect = AnimationData.parseData(
        AnimationStr.weapon_lightning_5,
      );

    }
};

WeaponAttack.prototype.callBack = function () {
  this.currentFrame++;

  if (this.currentFrame == 1)
    ((game.moveSet = game.current_FightSet.MapData),
      (game.moveSet.dir = this.reverseSide),
      (game.isMoveBg = true));

  if (
    this.currentFrame == 2 &&
    ((game.fightInfo.continueHurt = true),
    this.weaponId == 1017 || this.weaponId == 1217 || this.weaponId == 1117)
  )
    game.fightInfo.weaponHurt = true;

  if (this.currentFrame >= this.totalFrame)
    this.currentFrame = this.totalFrame - 1;

  this.currentFrame == 7 &&
    game.fightController.notifying(StaticVars.getConstants("attackerMessage"));

  if (this.currentFrame > 6) game.isMoveBg = false;

};

WeaponAttack.prototype.drawer = function (a, d, b, c, e) {
  Drawable.drawSquirrel(
    a,
    d,
    b,
    c,
    this.reverseSide,
    this.currentFrame,
    this.weaponId,
    this.level,
    false,
    game.ssCache,
    this.reverseSide,
    e,
  );

};

WeaponAttack.prototype.drawEffect = function (a, d, b) {
  Drawable.drawEffect(
    this.effectData[this.currentFrame],
    a,
    d,
    game.weaponCache,
    this.reverseSide,
    b,
  );

  this.SpecialEffect != void 0 &&
    Drawable.drawEffect(
      this.SpecialEffect[this.currentFrame],
      a,
      d,
      this.cache,
      this.reverseSide,
      b,
    );

};

var tl_common_attack = function () {
  this.reverseSide = false;

  Npc.call(this, 0);

  this.effectData = AnimationData.parseNPCData(
    AnimationStr.tl_effect_commonAttack,
  );

};

tl_common_attack.prototype.callBack = function () {
  this.currentFrame++;

  if (this.currentFrame == 1)
    ((game.moveSet = game.current_FightSet.MapData),
      (game.moveSet.dir = this.reverseSide),
      (game.isMoveBg = true));

  if (this.currentFrame == 2) game.fightInfo.continueHurt = true;

  if (this.currentFrame >= this.totalFrame)
    this.currentFrame = this.totalFrame - 1;

  this.currentFrame == 6 &&
    game.fightController.notifying(StaticVars.getConstants("attackerMessage"));

  if (this.currentFrame > 6) game.isMoveBg = false;

};

tl_common_attack.prototype.drawer = function (a, d, b, c, e) {
  Drawable.drawNPC(a, d, "tl", b, game.tlCache, e);

};

tl_common_attack.prototype.drawEffect = function (a, d, b) {
  Drawable.drawEffect(
    this.effectData[this.currentFrame],
    a,
    d,
    game.tlEffectCache,
    true,
    b,
  );

};

(function () {
  var a,
    d,
    b,
    c,
    e,
    f,
    g,
    i,
    j,
    h,
    n,
    s = 0,
    q = 0,
    m,
    k,
    o,
    r = function (a, d, h, n) {
      j = a;

      b = d;

      c = h;

      e = n.col || 1;

      f = n.row || 1;

      g = n.colGap || 10;

      i = n._rowGap || 10;

      s = n.left || 0;

      s *= game.scale;

      q = n.top || 0;

      q *= game.scale;

      m = n.itemWidth * game.scale || 100;

      k = n.itemHeight * game.scale || 100;

      o = n.currPage || 1;

    },
    p = function () {
      d = [];

      for (var a, b = 0;
 b < f * e;
 b++) j && ((a = j(b)), d.push(a));

      for (b = 0;
 b < f;

 c < e;
 c++)
          if ((a = d[b * e + c]))
            for (var h = 0;
 h < a.length;
 h++)
              ((a[h].x += s + c * m + g), (a[h].y += q + b * k + i));

    };

  r.prototype.setDataLength = function (d) {
    a = d;

    h == 0 && (h = 1);

    p();

      v(n - 1);

    };

    c.onPress = function () {
      v(n + 1);

    };

    b.visible = n > 1;

    c.visible = n < h;

  };

  r.prototype.refresh = function (b) {
    b && ((a = b), (h = Math.ceil(a / (f * e))));

    l();

  };

  };

    v(a);

  };

  r.prototype.getTotalPage = function () {
    return h;

  };

  r.prototype.getPageInfo = function () {
    return n + "/" + h;

  };

  var v = function (a) {
      n = Math.max(1, a);

      n = Math.min(h, n);

      b.visible = n > 1;

      c.visible = n < h;

      l();

    },
    l = function () {

        ? ((n = h), (b.visible = n > 1), (c.visible = n < h), (g = a))
